# M2 T24 S1 - full responsibility and evidence census

## Scope and limits

The owner expanded this audit to all integrated nodes and every responsibility
of T/S tasks preceding T24 S1, with audit/checklist marking only and no repair.
The search conservatively covers **all 1,992 unique inventory labels**. This
prevents an omitted mapping or a historical task's forgotten continuation from
escaping review. Source-line slices assign responsibility, not implementation
or conformance. The source index contains data as well as executable nodes.

All labels/line pairs were reconciled with the local listing, and every local
instruction span passed ROM opcode/address alignment. This is a mechanical
address check, not a branch/write equivalence proof. The initial 77 nodes have
the deeper [individual source/C/probe audit](m2-t24-s1-node-verification.md).
The other 1,915 received responsibility/evidence checks and remain without a
complete individual semantic certificate. They must not be reported as 1,915
newly verified implementations.

## Evidence dimensions

- Current C files explicitly mention 435 distinct exact inventory names.
  Comments and references are discovery clues, not proof of an integrated body.
- Prior history/proposal/map documents explicitly mention 340 distinct names.
  Whole-slice responsibilities additionally cover names those documents omit.
- Existing T14/T15 label tables explicitly map 62 additional names to C owners;
  these mappings were missing from the canonical checklist and are backfilled.
  T22 S1/P2 supplies 27 more explicit mappings, and current BulletBillHandler
  supplies one actor mapping: **90 additional mappings**, all still incomplete.
- The six bounded source routes execute 857 code-label entries. A PC hit is
  source execution, not proof that the corresponding native path is equivalent.
  Castle has residual output differences; several important routes never run.
- Existing x86 and x64 test binaries each passed 83/83 tests across the full run
  and a six-test retry. Those six Python tests first failed due to temporary
  directory permissions; they passed with the same tests and temporary output
  confined to the ignored audit directory. No expectations were changed.
- No further completion is inferred from these counts. Per-node semantics,
  all required branch/write cases, consumer proof for data, and relevant clean
  source/native route evidence are still required.

## Additional concrete scope findings

The T22 S1/P2 map distinguishes an existing BulletBillHandler actor from the
absent ProcessCannons scheduler, and shared player jump parameters from absent
ProcessWhirlpools/WhirlpoolActivate producers. Inspection of current frame
dispatch, objects.c and player.c confirms these missing game paths. The three
exact entry labels are marked implementation missing; related descendants
remain in the responsibility ledger, never silently passed. This is an audit
finding only. Existing cannon artwork/parser tests are not scheduler tests.

## Revision boundary

The first cohort's source fingerprint and artifact identities are recorded in
its report. Subsequent working-tree changes to fireball_core.c, fireball_spawn.c,
bubble.c and the fireball smoke test appeared during audit; the audit neither made nor reverted it. D1 remains
a valid finding on the recorded snapshot, but ProcFireball_Bubble and FireballObjCore now require
revalidation of the changed implementation. It is not counted as repaired or
complete. The three offscreen completions have no changed direct source owner.

## Responsibility totals

The partition follows the existing structural-recovery source slices and the
InitializeMemory exception. Physical slice assignment does not override the
cross-slice collaborators recorded in each row. Screen and dispatcher plans
are still unnumbered candidates; historical T10/T8 evidence is not a fabricated
new admission. Tables require consumer ownership in addition to this partition.

## Previous numeric-task coverage

All previous M2 T identifiers were reconciled as well as the individual S/P
sections below. Historical broad completion claims never promote their whole
scope. The following tasks retain overlapping evidence responsibilities; they
are not summed as disjoint node counts.

| Previous task | Audited responsibility / evidence disposition |
| --- | --- |
| T1 | Entire PRG and 1,992-label inventory; static alignment passes, not runtime equivalence. |
| T2 | Title/input/start and Continue; covered by T15/root rows and retained title-start history. |
| T3 | Area bootstrap/parser/background; covered by T18 and screen rows, including undocumented continuations. |
| T4 | Player movement/world collision; covered by T23/T17 rows; historic checkpoints do not close branches. |
| T5 | Blocks/items/enemies/projectiles/platforms/score; covered by T22/T19/T20/T17 and OAM collaborators. |
| T6 | Death/restart/warp/terminal modes; covered by T15 and player/collision/endgame rows. |
| T7 | Audio queues/channels/music; every T21 source-slice row included. |
| T8 | End-to-end native route/oracle; broad route assertion does not certify all GameEngine descendants. |
| T9 | Frame-output recorder and output ownership; evidence infrastructure, no automatic node matches. |
| T10 | All background output, including still-unnumbered screen/status candidate; included, not omitted for lack of a later T. |
| T11 | All OAM families; T16's physical line slice alone is insufficient, so all T17/T19 graphics rows and current C references are also searched. |
| T12 | Win32 consumer/controller boundary; CURRENT/QUEUE and reopened-frame proposal have evidence, but no separate numeric history record was found. Does not establish ROM-label matches. |
| T13 | Frame oracle; QUEUE calls it active and the reopened-frame proposal gives broad routes; no separate closure or exact S label list was found. Gap retained. |
| T14 | Current root proposal/map assigns 40 labels. An older reopened-frame proposal calls T14 cross-width proof; this historical identifier conflict is recorded, not silently used as proof. |
| T15 | Title/terminal proposal, S1 map and S2-S4 evidence sections; all exact mentions and unnamed slice labels included. |
| T16 | OAM/offscreen proposal S1-S3 and cross-slice writers/consumers; all named evidence included. |
| T17 | World/collision proposal S1-S6; movement 7555-7784 and bounding/OAM collaborators included in addition to its physical slice. |
| T18 | Area/parser S1-S3; InitializeMemory belongs to T14, block-metatile caller and star-flag collaborator retained. |
| T19 | Enemy stream/actors S1-S5; all recorded P sections, endgame actors and cross-slice claims included. |
| T20 | Fireballs/bubbles S1-S3 evidence; current source changed after first probes, so affected nodes require revalidation. |
| T21 | Audio S1/S2/S5 evidence; planned remaining channels remain gaps, not completed S claims. |
| T22 | Blocks/items S1/S2/S4 evidence and label-owner map; all continuations included even where exact names were omitted. |
| T23 | Player S1/S2 friction evidence plus full declared responsibility; no whole-slice promotion. |

Governance Td work supplies scope/accounting only and establishes no gameplay
matches. M1 static-generation/title work and M3/M4 host/presentation work do
not bypass this M2 ROM-label checklist. No historical task is marked conformant
because its heading says complete. Conflicting planned/active/complete S
headings remain an audit finding; their authority needs later consolidation.

## Individual evidence ledger

Each exact label appears once. C/test entries are literal-reference locations;
absence means no exact-name reference was found, not proof the behavior is
absent. H entries identify prior task sections. Source PC columns list the
routes with entry hits; B is conditional branches with both successors seen
over the six routes / local conditional branches. Data/alias labels do not
receive executable coverage. No automatic promotion follows a B value.

## Audit checks and retention

The accounting and documentation gates pass, including exact equality of the
1,992 inventory/census label-line pairs, individual status and anchor checks,
and named pending/completed lists. The expanded admission records all 1,992
exact labels, baseline three and zero additional forecast matches. Diff
whitespace checks pass. Enabling/disabling aggregate PC diagnostics produces
an identical ten-frame reference trace (SHA-256
`bd1c886aa0c8a20d2d6abdf79a0a696e6d4d476d9f6ba249195c82f19fd87530`).
Twenty audit-owned raw frame files were deleted after neutral summarization;
aggregate PC counts and audit harnesses remain in ignored build output.
No other task trace was removed. Product and test-expectation changes appearing
concurrently are preserved and are not audit repairs or audit-certified fixes.
